- **funessian-process-non-markovian**: Funessian过程：一种连续时间正可分非马尔可夫过程，具有初始状态记忆。平稳态下关联函数指数衰减（通常被视为马尔可夫特征），但记忆贯穿演化。互信息作为非马尔可夫性度量。应用于随机游走，展示记忆效应打破遍历性并改变扩散系数。 ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/neuroscience/funessian-process-non-markovian) · ★4 · 2 copies)

- **funfun-gem-5b6cde54aa**: 用於「FunFun.AI-故事語織小幫手」相關請求。您好！為了創作一個結合特定語詞且關於名人（偉人）的故事，我需要您提供以下資訊： 請您提供一組語詞： 這些語詞將會在故事中以加粗的方式呈現，並成為故事的核心元素，請您選擇幾個對您來說有意義或能激發靈感的詞語。 請您選擇一位名人（偉人）： 故事將會圍繞這位名人的生平或重要事蹟展開。 請您告知閱讀學生的年級： 這能幫助我調整故事的用詞與內容，使其更符合學生的理解程度。 提供這些資訊後，我將會為您創作一篇生動且富含啟發的故事，敬請期待！ ([chunsheng612/FunFun.AI-Website](https://github.com/chunsheng612/FunFun.AI-Website/tree/HEAD/skills/funfun-gem-5b6cde54aa) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **funfun-gem-f84b18eb96**: 用於「FunFun.AI-角色定裝師」相關請求。幫使用者建立「角色設定卡」，讓後續每一張分鏡圖中的角色外貌、服裝、年齡感與主要特徵可以保持一致。 ([chunsheng612/FunFun.AI-Website](https://github.com/chunsheng612/FunFun.AI-Website/tree/HEAD/skills/funfun-gem-f84b18eb96) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **funfun-gem-fb07b9cf00**: 用於「FunFun.AI-圖像提示卡教材(特殊教育)」相關請求。請幫我設計一組「上課準備」圖像提示卡。 請幫我把小組討論規則做成可列印的圖卡。 學生轉換活動時容易混亂，請幫我設計流程卡。 ([chunsheng612/FunFun.AI-Website](https://github.com/chunsheng612/FunFun.AI-Website/tree/HEAD/spark-skills/funfun-gem-fb07b9cf00) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **funfun-gem-fbb7c41f06**: 用於「FunFun.AI-英文俚語漫畫專家」相關請求。你是一位兼具「深厚歐美文化素養」與「頂尖兒童繪本專長」的 AI 教學專家。你的核心任務是將英文俚語還原為真實的文化背景或起源故事，並編寫、繪製出劇情流暢、排版靈活的「Q版歐美繪本風」兒童連環漫畫。你一定要呈現每一個步驟，絕對不可以直接出圖。 ([chunsheng612/FunFun.AI-Website](https://github.com/chunsheng612/FunFun.AI-Website/tree/HEAD/spark-skills/funfun-gem-fbb7c41f06) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **funfun-gem-fdad003f18**: 用於「FunFun.AI-數學錯誤分析與再教學(特殊教育)」相關請求。請幫我分析學生這題數學錯在哪裡，並設計再教學活動。 學生應用題一直列錯式，請幫我分析可能原因並產出追問句。 請幫我把這份數學作業的錯誤整理成小組補救教材。 ([chunsheng612/FunFun.AI-Website](https://github.com/chunsheng612/FunFun.AI-Website/tree/HEAD/spark-skills/funfun-gem-fdad003f18) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **funfun-gem-ff7d4f6e46**: 用於「國小五年級社會探究學習」相關請求。你是一位專門引導國小五年級學生的「社會科探究領航員」。你的任務不是直接告訴學生正確答案，而是透過提問、提供線索與鼓勵，引導他們完成「發現問題、建立假設、蒐集資料、論證說明」的探究過程。 ([chunsheng612/FunFun.AI-Website](https://github.com/chunsheng612/FunFun.AI-Website/tree/HEAD/skills/funfun-gem-ff7d4f6e46) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **fungal-abundance-association-analysis**: Use when you have a fungal taxonomic or functional abundance feature table (e.g., FunOMIC-T/FunOMIC-P profiles) joined to sample metadata and need to find which species associate with variables like country, body site, or disease cohort. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fungal-abundance-association-analysis))
- **fungal-bacterial-taxonomic-profile-harmonization**: Use when paired fungal and bacterial taxonomic abundance tables (e.g. FunOMIC-T output and MetaPhlAn profiles) from the same metagenomes must be subset to matched cohorts and combined for inter-kingdom correlation analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fungal-bacterial-taxonomic-profile-harmonization))
- **fungal-database-specificity-assessment**: Use when validating that a fungal marker-gene database does not falsely map bacterial sequencing reads — i.e., you need a specificity metric for a taxonomic profiler before applying it to mixed metagenomic samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fungal-database-specificity-assessment))
- **fungal-detection-rate-summary-statistics**: Use when profiling fungal taxonomic presence across a large cohort of human shotgun metagenomes and you need cohort-level detection rates and per-species prevalence tables (e.g. prevalence of S. cerevisiae across thousands of samples). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fungal-detection-rate-summary-statistics))
- **fungal-functional-group-classification**: Use when you have clustered eukaryotic (ITS) OTU sequences from metabarcoding data and need to know which OTUs correspond to known species and how those identified OTUs split across Fungi/Plantae/Animalia and fungal functional groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/fungal-functional-group-classification))
- **fungal-functional-profiling**: Use when performing fungal functional profiling of shotgun metagenomic reads against the FunOMIC-P protein database, especially when distinguishing fungal signal from dominant bacterial backgrounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fungal-functional-profiling))
- **fungal-genomics**: Use when assembling, annotating, or comparing fungal genomes (yeast, molds, plant and human pathogens). Evidence is thin; ask. Not for bacterial genomes (bacterial-genome-assembly-annotation) or amplicon profiling (amplicon-metabarcoding-analysis). ([oumarkme/research_agents](https://github.com/oumarkme/research_agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/bioinfo-lab/skills/fungal-genomics))
- **fungal-marker-gene-read-mapping**: Use when shotgun metagenomic FASTQ samples need fungal taxonomic profiling (species detection rates, prevalence, abundance tables) via read mapping to a curated fungal marker-gene database rather than ITS amplicon-style analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fungal-marker-gene-read-mapping))
- **fungal-protein-database-alignment**: Use when metagenomic sequencing reads must be assigned fungal functions via a fungal protein database, and the Diamond blastx threshold combination (identity, read coverage, e-value) needs to be selected or applied for fungal functional profiling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fungal-protein-database-alignment))
- **fungal-single-copy-marker-gene-identification**: Use when constructing a fungal taxonomic marker-gene database from a pool of assembled, quality-filtered fungal genomes (e.g., ~4,839 NCBI/JGI MycoCosm genomes) and you need per-genome single-copy ortholog extraction and redundancy removal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fungal-single-copy-marker-gene-identification))
- **fungal-taxonomic-database-construction**: Use when building a strain/species-level fungal taxonomic reference database for read-mapping mycobiome profiling from NCBI/JGI fungal genome assemblies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fungal-taxonomic-database-construction))
- **fungal-taxonomy-assignment-against-unite**: Use when the input is paired-end Illumina .fastq/.fastq.gz reads from fungal ITS marker-gene amplicon sequencing and a taxonomy-assigned OTU/ASV table with per-sample abundances is required, e.g. when reproducing the PEMA ITS mock-community evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/fungal-taxonomy-assignment-against-unite))
- **fungal-taxonomy-classification-against-unite**: Use when ASVs derived from fungal ITS2 amplicon sequencing (e.g., after DADA2 denoising and ITSx filtering) need genus/species-level taxonomic assignment, as in mock community validation or microbial ecology studies of fungi. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/fungal-taxonomy-classification-against-unite))
- **fungi-identification**: Feldbestimmung von Pilzen anhand morphologischer Merkmale, Sporenabdrucke, Lebensraumanalyse und saisonalem Kontext mit einem Sicherheit-zuerst-Ansatz. Umfasst Hut-, Lamellen-, Stiel- und Sporeneigenschaften, Verwechslungspartner- Differenzierung, Toxizitaetsrisikobewertung und die kritische Regel absoluter Sicherheit vor dem Verzehr. Verwenden beim Antreffen eines unbekannten Pilzes, beim Sammeln essbarer Pilze und der Notwendigkeit die Art vor dem Verzehr zu bestaetigen, beim Beurteilen ob Pilze in Garten oder auf dem Grundstueck schaedlich sind oder beim Unterscheiden einer essbaren Art vo… ([pjt222/agent-almanac](https://github.com/pjt222/agent-almanac/tree/HEAD/i18n/de/skills/fungi-identification) · ★45)
- **fungi-identification**: Field identification of fungi using morphological features, spore prints, habitat analysis, and seasonal context with a safety-first approach. Covers cap, gill, stem, and spore characteristics, look-alike differentiation, toxicity risk assessment, and the critical rule of absolute certainty before consumption. Use when encountering an unknown fungus, foraging for edible mushrooms and needing to confirm species before consumption, assessing whether fungi in a garden or property are harmful, or differentiating an edible species from a dangerous look-alike. ([pjt222/agent-almanac](https://github.com/pjt222/agent-almanac/tree/HEAD/skills/fungi-identification) · ★45 · 8 copies)
- **fungi-identification**: Identificación de hongos en campo usando características morfológicas, impresiones de esporas, análisis de hábitat y contexto estacional con un enfoque de seguridad primero. Cubre características de sombrero, láminas, pie y esporas, diferenciación de similares, evaluación de riesgo de toxicidad, y la regla crítica de certeza absoluta antes del consumo. Usar al encontrar un hongo desconocido, al recolectar setas comestibles y necesitar confirmar la especie antes del consumo, al evaluar si los hongos en un jardín o propiedad son dañinos, o al diferenciar una especie comestible de un similar pel… ([pjt222/agent-almanac](https://github.com/pjt222/agent-almanac/tree/HEAD/i18n/es/skills/fungi-identification) · ★45)
- **fungi-identification**: 使用形态特征、孢子印迹、栖息地分析和季节背景进行真菌的野外鉴定，采用安全第一 的方法。涵盖菌盖、菌褶、菌柄和孢子特征、相似种区分、毒性风险评估，以及食用前 必须绝对确定的关键规则。适用于遇到未知真菌时、采集食用蘑菇需要在食用前确认物种时、 评估花园或房产中的真菌是否有害时，或区分可食用物种与危险的相似种时。 ([pjt222/agent-almanac](https://github.com/pjt222/agent-almanac/tree/HEAD/i18n/zh-CN/skills/fungi-identification) · ★45)
- **fungi-identification**: 形態学的特徴、胞子紋、生息地分析、季節的文脈を使用した安全第一アプローチによる 菌類のフィールド同定。傘、ひだ、柄、胞子の特徴、類似種の区別、毒性リスク評価、 消費前の絶対的確実性の重要ルールを網羅する。未知の菌類に遭遇した時、食用キノコを 採集して消費前に種の確認が必要な時、庭や敷地の菌類が有害かどうかを評価する時、 または食用種と危険な類似種を区別する時に使用する。 ([pjt222/agent-almanac](https://github.com/pjt222/agent-almanac/tree/HEAD/i18n/ja/skills/fungi-identification) · ★45)
- **fungibilidade-e-duvida-objetiva**: Use para Fungibilidade e Dúvida Objetiva em sistema recursal, com análise de fatos, provas, teses, riscos, redação e resultado útil no Direito brasileiro. Não use para decisão final, assinatura, protocolo ou citação não verificada. ([bbpropulse/apsquad-dist](https://github.com/bbpropulse/apsquad-dist/tree/HEAD/skills/fungibilidade-e-duvida-objetiva))
- **fungibilidade-recursal**: Use para Fungibilidade Recursal em sistema recursal, com análise de fatos, provas, teses, riscos, redação e resultado útil no Direito brasileiro. Não use para decisão final, assinatura, protocolo ou citação não verificada. ([bbpropulse/apsquad-dist](https://github.com/bbpropulse/apsquad-dist/tree/HEAD/skills/fungibilidade-recursal))
- **fungible-asset-security**: Trigger FA_STANDARD flag detected (protocol uses FungibleAsset standard) - Used by Breadth agents, depth-token-flow ([PlamenTSV/plamen](https://github.com/PlamenTSV/plamen/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/aptos/fungible-asset-security) · ★316)
